# TOMATO / 番茄
# ===================================================================

## Tomato - Viral Disease (Tomato Spotted Wilt Virus) / 番茄 - 病毒病

[Tomato | Viral Disease] Q: What causes this?
A: Tomato spotted wilt is caused by Tomato Spotted Wilt Virus (TSWV), a tospovirus transmitted almost entirely by thrips, especially western flower thrips. Only immature thrips can acquire the virus, but the adults they grow into carry and spread it for the rest of their lives, so an early thrips infestation is what actually introduces the disease.

[番茄 | 病毒病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄斑萎病由番茄斑萎病毒（TSWV）引起，主要通过蓟马传播，尤其是西花蓟马。只有蓟马幼虫期才能获得病毒，但它们长为成虫后会终生携带并传播病毒，因此早期的蓟马侵染是该病爆发的关键原因。

[Tomato | Viral Disease] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for wilting combined with leaf curling and a purplish or bronze discoloration on young leaves. As the disease progresses, ring-shaped spots may appear on fruit, and plant growth becomes stunted and uneven. These symptoms together (not any single one) are what distinguish TSWV from ordinary wilting.

[番茄 | 病毒病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：注意萎蔫并伴随叶片卷曲，幼叶出现紫色或古铜色变色。病情发展后，果实上可能出现环状斑点，植株生长会变得矮小且不均匀。这些症状同时出现（而非单一症状）才是判断TSWV病毒病的关键依据。

[Tomato | Viral Disease] Q: How can I manage it?
A: There is no cure once a plant is infected, so management focuses on prevention: avoid planting near onion, garlic, or cereal fields since thrips build up heavily on them, use yellow sticky traps to monitor thrips populations, and remove and destroy infected plants immediately (rogue them out) to stop the virus from spreading to healthy plants nearby.

[番茄 | 病毒病] 问：如何防治？
答：植株一旦感染便无法治愈，因此防治重点在于预防：避免在洋葱、大蒜或谷类作物附近种植，因为蓟马容易在这些作物上大量繁殖；使用黄色粘虫板监测蓟马种群；发现感病植株应立即拔除销毁，以防病毒传播给周围健康植株。

[Tomato | Viral Disease] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season fruiting vegetable in the nightshade family, grown widely for fresh consumption and processing. It is highly sensitive to insect-vectored viruses, which is why thrips and aphid control is a routine part of tomato pest management programs, separate from managing fungal or bacterial diseases.

[番茄 | 病毒病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是茄科的一种喜温果菜，广泛用于鲜食和加工。番茄对昆虫传播的病毒病非常敏感，因此蓟马和蚜虫的防治是番茄病虫害管理中的常规工作，这与防治真菌或细菌性病害是分开进行的。

## Tomato - Blight / 番茄 - 疫病

[Tomato | Blight] Q: What causes this?
A: Blight in tomato (疫病) is commonly caused by Phytophthora species, water-mold pathogens that thrive in wet, poorly-drained soil and spread rapidly during periods of rain or overhead irrigation. Infection usually starts at the stem base or roots and moves upward through the plant.

[番茄 | 疫病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄疫病通常由疫霉菌（Phytophthora）引起，这是一种在潮湿、排水不良的土壤中容易滋生的病原菌，在多雨或喷灌频繁的条件下传播迅速。感染通常从茎基部或根部开始，逐渐向上蔓延。

[Tomato | Blight] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Early signs include dark, water-soaked lesions on stems near the soil line and sudden wilting of the whole plant even though the soil is moist. As the disease advances, the stem base becomes soft and discolored, and the plant can collapse within days under warm, wet conditions.

[番茄 | 疫病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：早期症状包括近地面茎部出现暗色水浸状病斑，即使土壤湿润植株也会突然萎蔫。病情加重后，茎基部变软并变色，在温暖潮湿条件下植株可能在几天内整株倒伏。

[Tomato | Blight] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Improve drainage and avoid overhead irrigation to reduce the wet conditions the pathogen needs, rotate away from tomato and other nightshades for at least two to three years in affected fields, and remove infected plants promptly since the pathogen survives in soil and plant debris.

[番茄 | 疫病] 问：如何防治？
答：改善田间排水，避免喷灌，以减少病原菌所需的潮湿环境；受害地块应与番茄及其他茄科作物轮作至少两到三年；及时清除病株，因为病原菌可在土壤和植株残体中长期存活。

[Tomato | Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato performs best in well-drained soil with consistent but not excessive moisture. Because several of its most serious diseases, including blight, are driven by wet conditions at the root zone, drainage and irrigation management are as important to tomato health as pest control.

[番茄 | 疫病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄在排水良好、水分适中但不过湿的土壤中长势最佳。由于包括疫病在内的多种严重病害都与根部积水有关，因此排水和灌溉管理对番茄的健康与虫害防治同样重要。

## Tomato - Bacterial Disease / 番茄 - 细菌性

[Tomato | Bacterial Disease] Q: What causes this?
A: Bacterial spot on tomato is caused by Xanthomonas bacteria, which are seed-borne and can also survive on infected transplants without showing symptoms until conditions favor an outbreak. Warm temperatures above 20°C combined with high humidity or splashing water (rain or overhead irrigation) are what trigger visible disease.

[番茄 | 细菌性] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄细菌性斑点病由黄单胞菌（Xanthomonas）引起，该病原可通过种子传播，也能在感病但未表现症状的种苗上潜伏，直到条件适宜时才爆发。20摄氏度以上的温度配合高湿度或雨水、喷灌溅水，是诱发明显病害的主要条件。

[Tomato | Bacterial Disease] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for small, dark, water-soaked spots on leaves that turn brown to black and feel slightly raised; leaf spot centers often dry out and tear, giving leaves a ragged look. On fruit, spots start small and scabby and can enlarge and merge as the disease progresses.

[番茄 | 细菌性] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：注意叶片上出现细小、暗色、水浸状斑点，随后变为褐色至黑色并略微隆起；病斑中心常常干裂，使叶片呈现破损状。果实上的病斑起初细小粗糙，病情加重后会扩大并连合。

[Tomato | Bacterial Disease] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use pathogen-free seed and disease-free transplants whenever possible, since this is the single most effective way to prevent an outbreak. Preventive copper sprays combined with these cultural practices help manage the disease once conditions become favorable, and avoiding overhead watering reduces bacterial spread between plants.

[番茄 | 细菌性] 问：如何防治？
答：尽可能使用无病原种子和健康种苗，这是预防该病最有效的方法。在条件适宜发病时，配合预防性喷施铜制剂可帮助控制病情；避免喷灌浇水可减少细菌在植株间的传播。

[Tomato | Bacterial Disease] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is grown in a wide range of climates but is particularly vulnerable to bacterial diseases during warm, humid growing seasons. Because the bacteria that cause spot diseases can hide symptomlessly in transplants, sourcing clean seedlings is one of the most important tomato-growing decisions a farmer makes.

[番茄 | 细菌性] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄适应多种气候种植，但在温暖潮湿的生长季节中尤其容易感染细菌性病害。由于引起斑点病的细菌可在种苗上无症状潜伏，因此选用干净健康的种苗是种植番茄时最重要的决定之一。

## Tomato - Blossom end rot / 番茄 - 脐腐病

[Tomato | Blossom end rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Blossom end rot is a physiological disorder, not an infectious disease. It develops when rapidly growing fruit does not receive enough calcium, often because irregular soil moisture, root stress, high salts, or overly rapid growth disrupt calcium movement into the fruit.

[番茄 | 脐腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄脐腐病是一种生理性障碍，并非传染性病害。快速发育的果实得不到足够钙时会发生，常与土壤水分忽干忽湿、根系受压、盐分过高或生长过快影响钙运输有关。

[Tomato | Blossom end rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for a pale or water-soaked spot at the blossom end of young fruit. It enlarges and becomes brown to black, sunken, dry, and leathery; secondary rots may later invade the damaged tissue.

[番茄 | 脐腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：幼果花端先出现浅色或水渍状斑点，随后扩大并变成褐色至黑色、凹陷、干燥和革质状，受损组织之后可能继发腐烂。

[Tomato | Blossom end rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Keep soil moisture even, mulch where appropriate, avoid root injury and excessive fertilization, and use a soil test before adding calcium. Pesticides do not cure blossom end rot, and damaged fruit will not recover.

[番茄 | 脐腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：保持土壤水分稳定，可适当覆盖地表；避免伤根和过量施肥。补钙前应先进行土壤检测。农药不能治愈脐腐病，已经受害的果实也不会恢复。

[Tomato | Blossom end rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure.

[番茄 | 脐腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。

## Tomato - Early Blight / 番茄 - 早疫病

[Tomato | Early Blight] Q: What causes this?
A: Early blight is caused by Alternaria solani, which survives on infected crop residue and related weeds. Spores spread by wind and splashing water, and infection is favored by prolonged leaf wetness and humid weather.

[番茄 | 早疫病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄早疫病主要由茄链格孢菌（Alternaria solani）引起，病原可在病残体和茄科杂草上存活，孢子通过风和溅水传播，叶片长时间潮湿和高湿有利于发病。

[Tomato | Early Blight] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Older leaves usually develop brown to black spots with concentric rings, often described as target-like lesions. Severe infection causes yellowing and leaf loss; fruit lesions are typically dark, dry, sunken, and may also show rings.

[番茄 | 早疫病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：老叶通常先出现褐色至黑色病斑，常具有同心轮纹，呈靶纹状。严重时叶片黄化脱落，果实可出现暗色、干燥、凹陷并带轮纹的病斑。

[Tomato | Early Blight] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Remove infected debris and volunteer nightshades, rotate away from tomato and potato, reduce prolonged leaf wetness, and use locally registered fungicides only when disease pressure and local guidance justify treatment.

[番茄 | 早疫病] 问：如何防治？
答：清除病残体和自生茄科植株，与番茄和马铃薯等作物轮作，减少叶片长时间潮湿；在病害压力较高且当地建议需要时，再使用登记允许的杀菌剂。

[Tomato | Early Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure.

[番茄 | 早疫病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。

## Tomato - Late Blight / 番茄 - 晚疫病

[Tomato | Late Blight] Q: What causes this?
A: Late blight is caused by Phytophthora infestans, an oomycete that spreads rapidly in cool, wet conditions through airborne spores and infected plant material.

[番茄 | 晚疫病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄晚疫病由疫霉菌 Phytophthora infestans 引起，在凉爽潮湿条件下可通过空气传播的孢子和带病植株材料迅速扩散。

[Tomato | Late Blight] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Leaves develop rapidly enlarging water-soaked or brown lesions, often with pale or whitish sporulation near lesion margins in humid weather. Stems can darken, and fruit may develop firm brown lesions.

[番茄 | 晚疫病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片出现快速扩大的水渍状或褐色病斑，潮湿时病斑边缘可见浅色或白色霉状物；茎部可变暗，果实可出现坚实褐色病斑。

[Tomato | Late Blight] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use healthy transplants, remove infected plants promptly, avoid leaving cull piles or infected volunteer plants, improve airflow, and follow regional late-blight alerts and registered fungicide guidance where available.

[番茄 | 晚疫病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用健康种苗，及时清除病株和病果堆，清除带病自生植株，改善通风，并根据当地晚疫病预警和登记用药指南进行防治。

[Tomato | Late Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure.

[番茄 | 晚疫病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。

## Tomato - Powdery Mildew / 番茄 - 白粉病

[Tomato | Powdery Mildew] Q: What causes this?
A: Tomato powdery mildew is caused by powdery mildew fungi that spread by airborne spores. Disease is often favored by moderate temperatures, dense canopies, and conditions that support fungal growth even without free water on leaves.

[番茄 | 白粉病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄白粉病由白粉菌类真菌引起，孢子可随空气传播。适中温度、冠层过密等环境有利于病害发生，即使叶面没有明显积水也可发展。

[Tomato | Powdery Mildew] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for white powdery patches on leaves that enlarge over time, with associated yellowing, browning, and premature leaf drop when disease becomes severe.

[番茄 | 白粉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片上出现白色粉状斑块并逐渐扩大，严重时伴随黄化、褐变和提前落叶。

[Tomato | Powdery Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Improve plant spacing and airflow, remove heavily affected tissue where practical, avoid excessive nitrogen, and use resistant varieties or locally registered fungicides when available and justified.

[番茄 | 白粉病] 问：如何防治？
答：改善株距和通风，适当清除严重受害组织，避免氮肥过量；条件允许时选用抗病品种，并按当地建议使用登记杀菌剂。

[Tomato | Powdery Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure.

[番茄 | 白粉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。

## Tomato - Fusarium Wilt / 番茄 - 镰刀菌枯萎病

[Tomato | Fusarium Wilt] Q: What causes this?
A: Fusarium wilt is caused by soilborne Fusarium oxysporum strains that invade tomato roots and vascular tissue. The pathogen can persist in soil and is favored when susceptible plants are grown repeatedly in infested ground.

[番茄 | 镰刀菌枯萎病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄镰刀菌枯萎病由土传尖孢镰刀菌相关菌株引起，病原从根部侵入维管组织，可在土壤中长期存活，连续种植感病番茄会增加风险。

[Tomato | Fusarium Wilt] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Lower leaves yellow and wilt first, often beginning on one side of the plant. Wilting progresses upward, growth is stunted, and cutting the lower stem may reveal brown vascular discoloration.

[番茄 | 镰刀菌枯萎病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：下部叶片先黄化和萎蔫，常先发生在植株一侧，随后向上发展，植株生长受抑；切开近地面茎部可见维管组织褐变。

[Tomato | Fusarium Wilt] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use resistant cultivars and clean transplants, avoid moving contaminated soil, manage root stress, and rotate with nonhost crops where useful. Severely affected plants should be removed.

[番茄 | 镰刀菌枯萎病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用抗病品种和健康种苗，避免携带污染土壤，减少根系胁迫，并在条件适宜时与非寄主作物轮作。严重病株应及时清除。

[Tomato | Fusarium Wilt] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure.

[番茄 | 镰刀菌枯萎病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。

## Tomato - Whiteflies / 番茄 - 白粉虱

[Tomato | Whiteflies] Q: What causes this?
A: Whitefly infestations occur when small sap-feeding whiteflies colonize leaf undersides. Warm conditions favor rapid population growth, and some whitefly species can also transmit plant viruses.

[番茄 | 白粉虱] 问：病因是什么？
答：白粉虱侵染是由成虫和若虫在叶片背面刺吸汁液造成的。温暖条件下种群增长迅速，部分白粉虱种类还可传播植物病毒。

[Tomato | Whiteflies] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for tiny white adults that fly when leaves are disturbed, scale-like immature stages under leaves, sticky honeydew, sooty mold, yellowing, and reduced vigor.

[番茄 | 白粉虱] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：轻触叶片时可见小型白色成虫飞起，叶背有鳞片状若虫，并可能出现蜜露、煤污、叶片黄化和植株长势下降。

[Tomato | Whiteflies] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Monitor leaf undersides and sticky traps, remove heavily infested plant material, control weeds that host whiteflies, conserve natural enemies, and use selective controls when thresholds justify treatment.

[番茄 | 白粉虱] 问：如何防治？
答：检查叶片背面并使用粘虫板监测，清除重度受害组织和寄主杂草，保护天敌，在虫口密度达到防治需要时优先采用选择性措施。

[Tomato | Whiteflies] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure.

[番茄 | 白粉虱] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。
