## Apple Tree - Mite / 苹果 - 螨类危害

[Apple Tree | Mite] Q: What causes this?
A: Mite is an arthropod-pest diagnosis associated with Apple Tree. Pest pressure can vary with crop stage, weather, surrounding hosts, and natural-enemy activity; species-level confirmation is important before selecting a control.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Apple+Tree+Mite

[苹果 | 螨类危害] 问：病因是什么？
答：苹果的“螨类危害”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Apple+Tree+Mite

[Apple Tree | Mite] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Mite on Apple Tree, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Apple+Tree+Mite

[苹果 | 螨类危害] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株苹果，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Apple+Tree+Mite

[Apple Tree | Mite] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Mite on Apple Tree: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Apple+Tree+Mite

[苹果 | 螨类危害] 问：如何防治？
答：对苹果的螨类危害采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Apple+Tree+Mite

[Apple Tree | Mite] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Apple Tree is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Mite'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Apple+Tree+Mite

[苹果 | 螨类危害] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：苹果已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“螨类危害”。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Apple+Tree+Mite

## Apple Tree - Mosaic Disease / 苹果 - 花叶病

[Apple Tree | Mosaic Disease] Q: What causes this?
A: Mosaic Disease is a disease diagnosis associated with Apple Tree. Viral and vector-associated diseases require confirmation because visually similar symptoms may have different causes; management often depends on clean planting material, vector management, sanitation, and resistant material where available.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Apple+Tree+Mosaic+Disease

A: For suspected Brown Spot on Avocado Tree, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. [Bitter Melon | Root Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Root Rot is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Bitter Melon. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Bitter+Melon+Root+Rot

[苦瓜 | 根腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：苦瓜的“根腐病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Bitter+Melon+Root+Rot

[Bitter Melon | Root Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Root Rot on Bitter Melon, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Bitter+Melon+Root+Rot

[苦瓜 | 根腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株苦瓜，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Bitter+Melon+Root+Rot

[Bitter Melon | Root Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Root Rot on Bitter Melon: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Bitter+Melon+Root+Rot

答：对芹菜的斑枯病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/celery/

[Celery | Septoria Leaf Spot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Celery is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Septoria Leaf Spot'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/celery/

[芹菜 | 斑枯病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：芹菜已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“斑枯病”。

## Celery - Slime Mold / 芹菜 - 黏菌病

[Celery | Slime Mold] Q: What causes this?
A: Slime Mold is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Celery. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/celery/

[芹菜 | 黏菌病] 问：病因是什么？
答：芹菜的“黏菌病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/celery/

[Celery | Slime Mold] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Slime Mold on Celery, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/celery/

[芹菜 | 黏菌病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株芹菜，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/celery/

[Celery | Slime Mold] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Slime Mold on Celery: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/celery/

SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance

## Chinese Cabbage - Soft Rot / 白菜 - 软腐病

[Chinese Cabbage | Soft Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Soft Rot is a bacterial-disease diagnosis associated with Chinese Cabbage. Bacterial diseases are commonly influenced by infected planting material, crop residue, moisture, wounds, and splash or handling, although the exact epidemiology is condition-specific.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Chinese+Cabbage+Soft+Rot

[白菜 | 软腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：白菜的“软腐病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Chinese+Cabbage+Soft+Rot

[Chinese Cabbage | Soft Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Soft Rot on Chinese Cabbage, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Chinese+Cabbage+Soft+Rot

[白菜 | 软腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株白菜，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Chinese+Cabbage+Soft+Rot

[Chinese Cabbage | Soft Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Soft Rot on Chinese Cabbage: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Chinese+Cabbage+Soft+Rot

[白菜 | 软腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：对白菜的软腐病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Chinese+Cabbage+Soft+Rot

[Chinese Cabbage | Soft Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Chinese Cabbage is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Soft Rot'.

## Cilantro - Septoria Leaf Spot / 香菜 - 斑枯病

[Cilantro | Septoria Leaf Spot] Q: What causes this?
A: Septoria Leaf Spot is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Cilantro. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cilantro+Septoria+Leaf+Spot

[香菜 | 斑枯病] 问：病因是什么？
答：香菜的“斑枯病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cilantro+Septoria+Leaf+Spot

[Cilantro | Septoria Leaf Spot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Septoria Leaf Spot on Cilantro, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cilantro+Septoria+Leaf+Spot

[香菜 | 斑枯病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株香菜，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cilantro+Septoria+Leaf+Spot

[Cilantro | Septoria Leaf Spot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Septoria Leaf Spot on Cilantro: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cilantro+Septoria+Leaf+Spot

[香菜 | 斑枯病] 问：如何防治？
答：对香菜的斑枯病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cilantro+Septoria+Leaf+Spot

[Cilantro | Septoria Leaf Spot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cilantro is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Septoria Leaf Spot'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cilantro+Septoria+Leaf+Spot

[香菜 | 斑枯病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：香菜已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“斑枯病”。

答：柑橘的“树脂病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。

[Citrus | Citrus Gummosis] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Citrus Gummosis on Citrus, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/citrus/

[柑橘 | 树脂病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株柑橘，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/citrus/

[Citrus | Citrus Gummosis] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Citrus Gummosis on Citrus: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/citrus/

[柑橘 | 树脂病] 问：如何防治？
答：对柑橘的树脂病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/citrus/

[Citrus | Citrus Gummosis] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Citrus is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Citrus Gummosis'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/citrus/

[柑橘 | 树脂病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：柑橘已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“树脂病”。

答：检查多株玉米，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[Corn | Bollworm Damage] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Bollworm Damage on Corn: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[玉米 | 棉铃虫危害] 问：如何防治？
答：对玉米的棉铃虫危害采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[Corn | Bollworm Damage] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Corn is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Bollworm Damage'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[玉米 | 棉铃虫危害] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：玉米已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“棉铃虫危害”。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

## Corn - Brown Spot / 玉米 - 褐斑病

[Corn | Brown Spot] Q: What causes this?

SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[Corn | Brown Spot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Brown Spot on Corn, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.

答：对玉米的白粉病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[Corn | Powdery Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Corn is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Powdery Mildew'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[玉米 | 白粉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。

## Corn - Rough Dwarf Disease / 玉米 - 粗缩病

[Corn | Rough Dwarf Disease] Q: What causes this?
A: Rough Dwarf Disease is a disease diagnosis associated with Corn. Viral and vector-associated diseases require confirmation because visually similar symptoms may have different causes; management often depends on clean planting material, vector management, sanitation, and resistant material where available.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[玉米 | 粗缩病] 问：病因是什么？
答：玉米的“粗缩病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[Corn | Rough Dwarf Disease] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Rough Dwarf Disease on Corn, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[玉米 | 粗缩病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株玉米，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[Corn | Rough Dwarf Disease] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Rough Dwarf Disease on Corn: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[玉米 | 粗缩病] 问：如何防治？

## Corn - Top Rot / 玉米 - 顶腐病

[Corn | Top Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Top Rot is a bacterial-disease diagnosis associated with Corn. Bacterial diseases are commonly influenced by infected planting material, crop residue, moisture, wounds, and splash or handling, although the exact epidemiology is condition-specific.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[玉米 | 顶腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：玉米的“顶腐病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[Corn | Top Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Top Rot on Corn, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[玉米 | 顶腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株玉米，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[Corn | Top Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Top Rot on Corn: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[玉米 | 顶腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：对玉米的顶腐病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[Corn | Top Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Corn is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Top Rot'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[玉米 | 顶腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：玉米已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“顶腐病”。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

## Corn - Zinc Deficiency / 玉米 - 缺锌生理病害

[Corn | Zinc Deficiency] Q: What causes this?
A: Zinc Deficiency is a diagnosed disorder or damage category associated with Corn. Similar visible injury can arise from environmental, nutritional, chemical, or biological causes, so field history and distribution of symptoms are important for confirmation.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/corn/

[玉米 | 缺锌生理病害] 问：病因是什么？

Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cotton+Verticillium+Wilt

[棉花 | 黄萎病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株棉花，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cotton+Verticillium+Wilt

[Cotton | Verticillium Wilt] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Verticillium Wilt on Cotton: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cotton+Verticillium+Wilt

[棉花 | 黄萎病] 问：如何防治？
答：对棉花的黄萎病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cotton+Verticillium+Wilt

[Cotton | Verticillium Wilt] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cotton is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Verticillium Wilt'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cotton+Verticillium+Wilt

[棉花 | 黄萎病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：棉花已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“黄萎病”。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Cotton+Verticillium+Wilt

答：检查多株茄子，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/eggplant/

[Eggplant | Iron Deficiency] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Iron Deficiency on Eggplant: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/eggplant/

[茄子 | 缺铁] 问：如何防治？
答：对茄子的缺铁采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/eggplant/

[Eggplant | Iron Deficiency] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Eggplant is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Iron Deficiency'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/eggplant/

[茄子 | 缺铁] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：茄子已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“缺铁”。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/eggplant/

答：对葡萄的褐枯病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[Grape | Brown Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Grape is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Brown Blight'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[葡萄 | 褐枯病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：葡萄已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“褐枯病”。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

## Grape - Brown Spot / 葡萄 - 褐斑病

[Grape | Brown Spot] Q: What causes this?
A: Brown Spot is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Grape. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[葡萄 | 褐斑病] 问：病因是什么？
答：葡萄的“褐斑病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[Grape | Brown Spot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Brown Spot on Grape, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[葡萄 | 褐斑病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株葡萄，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[Grape | Brown Spot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Brown Spot on Grape: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[葡萄 | 疮痂病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：葡萄已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“疮痂病”。

## Grape - Sooty Mold / 葡萄 - 煤污病

[Grape | Sooty Mold] Q: What causes this?
A: Sooty Mold is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Grape. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[葡萄 | 煤污病] 问：病因是什么？
答：葡萄的“煤污病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[Grape | Sooty Mold] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Sooty Mold on Grape, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[葡萄 | 煤污病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株葡萄，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[Grape | Sooty Mold] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Sooty Mold on Grape: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

[葡萄 | 煤污病] 问：如何防治？
答：对葡萄的煤污病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/grape/

A: Grape is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Sooty Mold'.

## Kiwifruit - Plaster Disease / 猕猴桃 - 膏药病

[Kiwifruit | Plaster Disease] Q: What causes this?
A: Plaster Disease is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Kiwifruit. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Kiwifruit+Plaster+Disease

[猕猴桃 | 膏药病] 问：病因是什么？
答：猕猴桃的“膏药病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Kiwifruit+Plaster+Disease

[Kiwifruit | Plaster Disease] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Plaster Disease on Kiwifruit, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Kiwifruit+Plaster+Disease

[猕猴桃 | 膏药病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株猕猴桃，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Kiwifruit+Plaster+Disease

[Kiwifruit | Plaster Disease] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Plaster Disease on Kiwifruit: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Kiwifruit+Plaster+Disease

[猕猴桃 | 膏药病] 问：如何防治？
答：对猕猴桃的膏药病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Kiwifruit+Plaster+Disease

[Kiwifruit | Plaster Disease] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Kiwifruit is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Plaster Disease'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Kiwifruit+Plaster+Disease

## Loquat - Sunscald / 枇杷 - 日灼病

[Loquat | Sunscald] Q: What causes this?
A: Sunscald is a diagnosed disorder or damage category associated with Loquat. Similar visible injury can arise from environmental, nutritional, chemical, or biological causes, so field history and distribution of symptoms are important for confirmation.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance

[Loquat | Sunscald] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Sunscald on Loquat, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Loquat+Sunscald

[枇杷 | 日灼病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株枇杷，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Loquat+Sunscald

[Loquat | Sunscald] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Sunscald on Loquat: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Loquat+Sunscald

[枇杷 | 日灼病] 问：如何防治？
答：对枇杷的日灼病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。

[Melon | Angular Leaf Spot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Angular Leaf Spot on Melon, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Melon+Angular+Leaf+Spot

[甜瓜 | 细菌性角斑病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株甜瓜，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Melon+Angular+Leaf+Spot

[Melon | Angular Leaf Spot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Angular Leaf Spot on Melon: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Melon+Angular+Leaf+Spot

来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Money+Tree+Thrips

[Money Tree | Thrips] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Thrips on Money Tree, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Money+Tree+Thrips

[发财树 | 蓟马] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株发财树，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Money+Tree+Thrips

[Money Tree | Thrips] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Thrips on Money Tree: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Money+Tree+Thrips

[发财树 | 蓟马] 问：如何防治？
答：对发财树的蓟马采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Money+Tree+Thrips

[Money Tree | Thrips] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Money Tree is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Thrips'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Money+Tree+Thrips

[发财树 | 蓟马] 问：介绍一下这种作物。

Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.

[Pea | Brown Spot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Brown Spot on Pea, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pea+Root+Rot

[豌豆 | 根腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：对豌豆的根腐病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pea+Root+Rot

[Pea | Root Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pea is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Root Rot'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pea+Root+Rot

[豌豆 | 根腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：豌豆已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“根腐病”。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pea+Root+Rot

## Pea - Rust / 豌豆 - 锈病

[Pea | Rust] Q: What causes this?
A: Rust is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Pea. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pea+Rust

[豌豆 | 锈病] 问：病因是什么？
答：豌豆的“锈病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pea+Rust

[Pea | Rust] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Rust on Pea, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pea+Rust

[豌豆 | 锈病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株豌豆，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。

A: Peach Tree is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Root Rot'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Peach+Tree+Root+Rot

[桃 | 根腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：桃已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“根腐病”。

## Peach Tree - Shot Hole / 桃 - 穿孔病

[Peach Tree | Shot Hole] Q: What causes this?
A: Shot Hole is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Peach Tree. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Peach+Tree+Shot+Hole

[桃 | 穿孔病] 问：病因是什么？
答：桃的“穿孔病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Peach+Tree+Shot+Hole

[Peach Tree | Shot Hole] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Shot Hole on Peach Tree, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Peach+Tree+Shot+Hole

[桃 | 穿孔病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株桃，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Peach+Tree+Shot+Hole

[Peach Tree | Shot Hole] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Shot Hole on Peach Tree: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Peach+Tree+Shot+Hole

[桃 | 穿孔病] 问：如何防治？
答：对桃的穿孔病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Peach+Tree+Shot+Hole

答：梨的“干腐病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pear+Tree+Dry+Rot

[Pear Tree | Dry Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Dry Rot on Pear Tree, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pear+Tree+Dry+Rot

[梨 | 干腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株梨，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pear+Tree+Dry+Rot

[Pear Tree | Dry Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Dry Rot on Pear Tree: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pear+Tree+Dry+Rot

[梨 | 干腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：对梨的干腐病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pear+Tree+Dry+Rot

[Pear Tree | Dry Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pear Tree is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Dry Rot'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pear+Tree+Dry+Rot

[梨 | 干腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：梨已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“干腐病”。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance

答：检查多株柿子树，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Persimmon+Tree+Black+Spot

[Persimmon Tree | Black Spot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Black Spot on Persimmon Tree: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Persimmon+Tree+Black+Spot

[柿子树 | 黑斑病] 问：如何防治？
答：对柿子树的黑斑病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Persimmon+Tree+Black+Spot

[Persimmon Tree | Black Spot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Persimmon Tree is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Black Spot'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Persimmon+Tree+Black+Spot

[柿子树 | 黑斑病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。

## Potato - Anthracnose / 马铃薯 - 炭疽病

[Potato | Anthracnose] Q: What causes this?
A: Anthracnose is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Potato. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/potato/

[马铃薯 | 炭疽病] 问：病因是什么？
答：马铃薯的“炭疽病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/potato/

[Potato | Anthracnose] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Anthracnose on Potato, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/potato/

来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/potato/

[Potato | Mealybug Damage] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Mealybug Damage on Potato: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/potato/

[马铃薯 | 粉蚧类危害] 问：如何防治？
答：对马铃薯的粉蚧类危害采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/potato/

[Potato | Mealybug Damage] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Potato is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Mealybug Damage'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/potato/

[马铃薯 | 粉蚧类危害] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：马铃薯已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“粉蚧类危害”。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/potato/

## Potato - Ring Rot / 马铃薯 - 环腐病

[Potato | Ring Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Ring Rot is a bacterial-disease diagnosis associated with Potato. Bacterial diseases are commonly influenced by infected planting material, crop residue, moisture, wounds, and splash or handling, although the exact epidemiology is condition-specific.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/potato/

[马铃薯 | 环腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：马铃薯的“环腐病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/potato/

[Potato | Ring Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Ring Rot on Potato, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/potato/

[马铃薯 | 环腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株马铃薯，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。

来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pumpkin+Powdery+Mildew

[Pumpkin | Powdery Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Powdery Mildew on Pumpkin: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pumpkin+Powdery+Mildew

[南瓜 | 白粉病] 问：如何防治？
答：对南瓜的白粉病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pumpkin+Powdery+Mildew

[Pumpkin | Powdery Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pumpkin is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Powdery Mildew'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Pumpkin+Powdery+Mildew

[南瓜 | 白粉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：南瓜已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“白粉病”。

## Rapeseed - Anthracnose / 油菜 - 炭疽病

[Rapeseed | Anthracnose] Q: What causes this?
A: Anthracnose is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Rapeseed. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Rapeseed+Anthracnose

[油菜 | 炭疽病] 问：病因是什么？
答：油菜的“炭疽病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Rapeseed+Anthracnose

[Rapeseed | Anthracnose] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Anthracnose on Rapeseed, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Rapeseed+Anthracnose

来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[Rice | Bacterial Blight] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Bacterial Blight on Rice: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[水稻 | 白叶枯病] 问：如何防治？
答：对水稻的白叶枯病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[Rice | Bacterial Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Rice is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Bacterial Blight'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[水稻 | 白叶枯病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：水稻已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“白叶枯病”。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

## Rice - Brown Planthopper Damage / 水稻 - 稻飞虱危害

[Rice | Brown Planthopper Damage] Q: What causes this?
A: Brown Planthopper Damage is a diagnosed disorder or damage category associated with Rice. Similar visible injury can arise from environmental, nutritional, chemical, or biological causes, so field history and distribution of symptoms are important for confirmation.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[水稻 | 稻飞虱危害] 问：病因是什么？
答：水稻的“稻飞虱危害”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[Rice | Brown Planthopper Damage] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Brown Planthopper Damage on Rice, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[水稻 | 稻飞虱危害] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株水稻，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines

SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[水稻 | 稻曲病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：水稻已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“稻曲病”。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

## Rice - Leaf Sheath Rot / 水稻 - 叶鞘腐败病

[Rice | Leaf Sheath Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Leaf Sheath Rot is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Rice. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[水稻 | 叶鞘腐败病] 问：病因是什么？
答：水稻的“叶鞘腐败病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[Rice | Leaf Sheath Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Leaf Sheath Rot on Rice, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[水稻 | 叶鞘腐败病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株水稻，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[Rice | Leaf Sheath Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Leaf Sheath Rot on Rice: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[水稻 | 叶鞘腐败病] 问：如何防治？
答：对水稻的叶鞘腐败病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[Rice | Leaf Sheath Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Rice is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Leaf Sheath Rot'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/rice/

[水稻 | 叶鞘腐败病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。

[月季/玫瑰 | 灰霉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株月季/玫瑰，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。

A: Use integrated pest management for Gray Mold on Rose: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Rose+Gray+Mold

来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Scallion+Downy+Mildew

[Scallion | Downy Mildew] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Downy Mildew on Scallion, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Scallion+Downy+Mildew

[葱 | 霜霉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株葱，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Scallion+Downy+Mildew

[Scallion | Downy Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Downy Mildew on Scallion: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Scallion+Downy+Mildew

[葱 | 霜霉病] 问：如何防治？
答：对葱的霜霉病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Scallion+Downy+Mildew

A: Scallion is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Downy Mildew'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Scallion+Downy+Mildew

Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Soybean+Anthracnose

[黄豆 | 炭疽病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株黄豆，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Soybean+Anthracnose

[Soybean | Anthracnose] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Anthracnose on Soybean: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Soybean+Anthracnose

[黄豆 | 炭疽病] 问：如何防治？
答：对黄豆的炭疽病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Soybean+Anthracnose

[Soybean | Anthracnose] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Soybean is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Anthracnose'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Soybean+Anthracnose

[黄豆 | 炭疽病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：黄豆已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“炭疽病”。

[菠菜 | 霜霉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株菠菜，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/spinach/

[Spinach | Downy Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Downy Mildew on Spinach: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/spinach/

[菠菜 | 霜霉病] 问：如何防治？
答：对菠菜的霜霉病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/spinach/

[Spinach | Downy Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Spinach is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Downy Mildew'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/spinach/

[菠菜 | 霜霉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：菠菜已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“霜霉病”。

来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/spinach/

A: For suspected Eriophyid Mite on Spinach, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. 答：检查多株草莓，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/strawberry/

[Strawberry | Iron Deficiency Chlorosis] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Iron Deficiency Chlorosis on Strawberry: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/strawberry/

[草莓 | 缺铁] 问：如何防治？
答：对草莓的缺铁采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/strawberry/

[Strawberry | Iron Deficiency Chlorosis] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Strawberry is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Iron Deficiency Chlorosis'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/strawberry/

[草莓 | 缺铁] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：草莓已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“缺铁”。

Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Succulent+Mealybug

[多肉 | 蚧壳虫] 问：如何防治？
答：对多肉的蚧壳虫采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。

SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance

[多肉 | 根腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：多肉的“根腐病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Succulent+Root+Rot

[Succulent | Root Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Root Rot on Succulent, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Succulent+Root+Rot

[多肉 | 根腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株多肉，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
