## Sweet Potato - Downy Mildew / 红薯 - 霜霉病

[Sweet Potato | Downy Mildew] Q: What causes this?
A: Downy Mildew is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Sweet Potato. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Sweet+Potato+Downy+Mildew

[红薯 | 霜霉病] 问：病因是什么？
答：红薯的“霜霉病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Sweet+Potato+Downy+Mildew

[Sweet Potato | Downy Mildew] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Downy Mildew on Sweet Potato, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Sweet+Potato+Downy+Mildew

[红薯 | 霜霉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株红薯，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Sweet+Potato+Downy+Mildew

[Sweet Potato | Downy Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Downy Mildew on Sweet Potato: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.

答：对茶的枯芽病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Tea+Bud+Blight

[Tea | Bud Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tea is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Bud Blight'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Tea+Bud+Blight

[茶 | 枯芽病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：茶已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“枯芽病”。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Tea+Bud+Blight

## Tobacco - Bacterial Wilt / 烟草 - 青枯病

[Tobacco | Bacterial Wilt] Q: What causes this?
A: Bacterial Wilt is a bacterial-disease diagnosis associated with Tobacco. Bacterial diseases are commonly influenced by infected planting material, crop residue, moisture, wounds, and splash or handling, although the exact epidemiology is condition-specific.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Tobacco+Bacterial+Wilt

[烟草 | 青枯病] 问：病因是什么？
答：烟草的“青枯病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Tobacco+Bacterial+Wilt

[Tobacco | Bacterial Wilt] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Bacterial Wilt on Tobacco, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Tobacco+Bacterial+Wilt

[烟草 | 青枯病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株烟草，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Tobacco+Bacterial+Wilt

[Tobacco | Bacterial Wilt] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Bacterial Wilt on Tobacco: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Tobacco+Bacterial+Wilt

[烟草 | 青枯病] 问：如何防治？

A: Tomato is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Anthracnose'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[番茄 | 炭疽病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“炭疽病”。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

## Tomato - Brown Spot / 番茄 - 褐斑病

[Tomato | Brown Spot] Q: What causes this?
A: Brown Spot is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Tomato. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[番茄 | 褐斑病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄的“褐斑病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[Tomato | Brown Spot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Brown Spot on Tomato, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[番茄 | 褐斑病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株番茄，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[Tomato | Brown Spot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Brown Spot on Tomato: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[番茄 | 褐斑病] 问：如何防治？
答：对番茄的褐斑病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[Tomato | Brown Spot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Brown Spot'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[番茄 | 轮纹斑病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄的“轮纹斑病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[Tomato | Ring Spot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Ring Spot on Tomato, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[番茄 | 轮纹斑病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株番茄，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[Tomato | Ring Spot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Ring Spot on Tomato: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[番茄 | 轮纹斑病] 问：如何防治？
答：对番茄的轮纹斑病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[Tomato | Ring Spot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Ring Spot'.
SOURCE: UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
SOURCE_URL: https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

[番茄 | 轮纹斑病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“轮纹斑病”。
来源：UC Statewide IPM Program — Agriculture Pest Management Guidelines
来源链接：https://ipm.ucanr.edu/agriculture/tomato/

SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Wheat+Glume+Blotch

[小麦 | 颖枯病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株小麦，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Wheat+Glume+Blotch

[Wheat | Glume Blotch] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Glume Blotch on Wheat: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Wheat+Glume+Blotch

[小麦 | 颖枯病] 问：如何防治？
答：对小麦的颖枯病采用综合管理：先确认诊断，再结合健康种苗、卫生措施、适当栽培管理、监测以及可用的抗性品种或非化学方法。任何农药必须遵循当地登记和产品标签，并依据下方来源做针对性决策。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Wheat+Glume+Blotch

[Wheat | Glume Blotch] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Wheat is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Glume Blotch'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Wheat+Glume+Blotch

[小麦 | 颖枯病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：小麦已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“颖枯病”。

A: For suspected Scale Insect on Wolfberry, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.

来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Yam+Spodoptera+Damage

[Yam | Spodoptera Damage] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Yam is included in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Reliable diagnosis combines visual symptoms with crop growth stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, field history, and symptom distribution. This record links the crop to the dataset condition label 'Spodoptera Damage'.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Yam+Spodoptera+Damage

[山药 | 斜纹夜蛾危害] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：山药已纳入AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。可靠诊断应综合视觉症状、生育阶段、天气和灌溉、害虫出现、田间历史以及症状分布。本条记录对应的数据集标签为“斜纹夜蛾危害”。

## Zucchini - Flower Rot / 西葫芦 - 花腐病

[Zucchini | Flower Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Flower Rot is a plant-disease diagnosis associated with Zucchini. Disease development depends on the causal organism plus a susceptible host and favorable environmental conditions; exact pathogen and epidemiology should be confirmed from the cited crop guidance.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Zucchini+Flower+Rot

[西葫芦 | 花腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：西葫芦的“花腐病”条目来自AgroMind扩展标签覆盖。病因应结合下方权威作物指南进一步确认，因为相似的视觉症状可能由不同病原、害虫或环境因素造成。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Zucchini+Flower+Rot

[Zucchini | Flower Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For suspected Flower Rot on Zucchini, compare multiple affected plants and inspect leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit as relevant. Record lesion or discoloration pattern, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding injury, abnormal growth, and where symptoms occur in the field; use the cited crop guidance to distinguish look-alike problems.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Zucchini+Flower+Rot

[西葫芦 | 花腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：检查多株西葫芦，并根据情况观察叶、茎、根、花和果实的斑点、变色、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长，同时记录症状在田间的分布，以便与下方来源中的相似问题区分。
来源：University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
来源链接：https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Zucchini+Flower+Rot

[Zucchini | Flower Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use integrated pest management for Flower Rot on Zucchini: confirm the diagnosis, use clean planting material, sanitation and crop-appropriate cultural practices, monitor disease or pest pressure, and favor resistant varieties or biological/nonchemical controls when available. Any pesticide use must follow the product label and local registration; the source below should be checked for condition-specific decisions.
SOURCE: University Extension verification lead — search required for condition-specific guidance
SOURCE_URL: https://extension.umn.edu/search?text=Zucchini+Flower+Rot
